const SCRIM_OP = NV["opacity.scrim"];
function scrim(f, x, y, w, h) { box(f, x, y, w, h, { fill: "palette.black", op: SCRIM_OP, opTok: "opacity.scrim", name: "modal-scrim", k: K() }); } // pp/361 r3's helper: by value at opacity.scrim (80-parts re-asserts it after the bind)

// ---- the T2 controls of the board being built, and the frame being drawn ----
const T2S = []; // { frame, x, y, w, h, labelX, reason, what }
const EST = []; // { frame, what, host: rect, kind } the estimated options and headers drawn (d8Checks rule 6)
let CUR_F = null;

// ---- PROPOSED stamps and margin notes (pp/241 R1 F-6 / F-10: on a color.surface.raised chip, space.2 apart) ----
let STAMPS = null; // { par, cols: [{ x, w, top? }], top, maxY, list }
function stamp(y, label, o) { if (STAMPS) STAMPS.list.push({ y, t: (o && o.plain ? "" : (o && o.owner ? OWNER : PROP) + " · ") + label, name: o && o.plain ? "annotation" : "proposed-stamp" }); }
function flushStamps() { // #176: a stamp that would not fit at its own y moves up (never above the one before it), so the column fills before it overflows
  let ci = 0, bottom = (STAMPS.cols[0].top != null ? STAMPS.cols[0].top : STAMPS.top) - 6;
  const list = STAMPS.list.sort((p, q) => p.y - q.y), hOf = (st, cl) => Math.ceil(estLines(st.t, Math.floor(cl.w * 0.85), TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP));
  const rest = list.map((st, i) => list.slice(i).reduce((a, s0) => a + hOf(s0, STAMPS.cols[0]) + 2 + 6, 0));
  for (const [i, st] of list.entries()) {
    for (;;) {
      const cl = STAMPS.cols[ci], h = hOf(st, cl), yy = Math.max(bottom + 6, Math.min(st.y, STAMPS.maxY - rest[i]));
      if (yy + h + 2 <= STAMPS.maxY) {
        const kk = K(); box(STAMPS.par, cl.x - 4, yy - 2, cl.w + 8, h + 4, { fill: "color.surface.raised", r: "radius.xs", name: "stamp-chip", k: kk });
        txt(STAMPS.par, st.t, cl.x, yy, cl.w, { type: CAP, col: PRI, k: kk, name: st.name, ml: true }); bottom = yy + h + 2 + NV["space.2"]; break;
      }
      if (++ci >= STAMPS.cols.length) { warn("stamp", CUR_F + ": no room for the stamp \"" + st.t.slice(0, 60) + "\""); return; }
      bottom = (STAMPS.cols[ci].top != null ? STAMPS.cols[ci].top : STAMPS.top) - 6;
    }
  }
}
function withStamps(par, cols, top, maxY, fn) { const p = STAMPS; STAMPS = { par, cols, top, maxY, list: [] }; try { const r = fn(); flushStamps(); return r; } finally { STAMPS = p; } }

// ---- the board layout (§0: one row of windows, 80 px apart; each caption prints the §16.1 row's cells) ----
const FRAME_EXTRA = []; // { board, row, name }: a frame drawn beside a register row that is not a row of its own (none on this board set)
function regCap(row, extra) { return row.frame + " · board " + row.board + " · " + row.theme + " · State: " + row.state + " · Components: " + row.components + " · Copy: " + row.copy + " · Tokens: " + row.tokens + " · Status: " + row.status + (extra ? " · " + extra : ""); }
function lay(B, items) {
  let x = B.X, bottom = B.y;
  for (const it of items) {
    const ex = it.draw(x, B.y) || "";
    const ch = caption(B, x, B.y + VH + 12, VW, it.cap ? it.cap : regCap(it.row, ex));
    bottom = Math.max(bottom, B.y + VH + 12 + ch);
    x += VW + FGAPX;
  }
  return bottom;
}
// the words a frame must draw for a Copy id: the W-row's strings, unless the frame draws the row with its own slot values or only the
// strings that belong to its state (WSUB, each entry says why); d8Checks rule 7 reads this
const WSUB = {};
const wordsFor = (row, id) => { const st = regState(row), s = WSUB[st] && WSUB[st][id]; return s || WD[id] || null; };
// =============================================================================================================
// SCR-104, the scanner pane (§2.2's anatomy, top to bottom): the panel frame and header (E15-D03's dockPanel, kind word "Scanner"), the
// filter bar (CMP-055: CMP-011 chips as two sibling buttons, Add filter, Rank by CMP-009, the preset name and Presets ▾ CMP-041), the
// derivation notes under estimated chips (§4.1), the notes (CMP-028: cap, live-only, inputs stale, removed criterion), the caption (W-21),
// the results grid (CMP-049 grid: §8.1's columns, compact 24 px rows, alternating surfaces, the sparkline CMP-024, CMP-227's look in
// the estimated headers, Status chips CMP-239 / Live only), the result states (CMP-026 / CMP-027), the footer (count, age, Pause
// updates, the sort hold, Send matches to watchlist…), and the overlays the frames open (menus CMP-041, the range editor CMP-017 with
// CMP-008 fields, CMP-043 / CMP-044 dialogs). The visual language is E08-D07's watchlist panel (its chrome, table rhythm and chips).
// =============================================================================================================
const PD = () => DZ("compact"); // §8.4: compact by default (16:113; D07 D30)
const IN = NV["space.inset.sm"], IN_XS = NV["space.inset.xs"]; // A-9: the filter bar binds space.inset.* (space.filterbar.* is not shipped)
const NUMS = "type.num.sm"; // E08-D07 §7.1: numbers type.num.sm (11 px, tabular) at compact density
const STALE_TXT = "color.data-confidence.stale.text", STALE_SURF = "color.data-confidence.stale.surface", STALE_IND = "color.data-confidence.stale.indicator";
let DN_SEQ = 0; // the derivation notes' follow-row ids

// ---- CMP-227's look, embedded (§3.3: presentational, no role, no tabindex, the glyph aria-hidden; `inline` `sm`, 15:2673) ----
const BADGE_WORD = "(estimated)";
function badgeW() { return Math.ceil(IN_XS + gw("≈") + IN_XS + chipTextW(BADGE_WORD, CAP) + IN_XS); }
function estBadge(par, x, y, h, k) { // fill color.status.info.subtle, outline border.hairline + glyph color.status.info.default, the word color.text.primary (15:2677)
  const w = badgeW();
  box(par, x, y, w, h, { fill: "color.status.info.subtle", stroke: "color.status.info.default", r: "radius.badge", name: "estimated-badge", k, role: "pill", sz: "no height token (CR-7)" });
  gly(par, "≈", x + IN_XS, y + (h - NV["size.icon.sm"] * 1.2) / 2, { col: "color.status.info.default", k, name: "estimated-glyph" });
  txt(par, BADGE_WORD.replace(/ /g, NBSP), x + IN_XS + gw("≈") + IN_XS, y + (h - th1(CAP)) / 2, chipTextW(BADGE_WORD, CAP), { type: CAP, col: PRI, k, name: "estimated-word" });
  return w;
}

// ---- CMP-011 chip (two sibling buttons: the label opens the popover, the remove IconButton named W-6's "Remove {criterion} filter") ----
const REMOVE_W = AN("target.min"); // 05:100: the remove button is a 24 × 24 target
function filterChipW(fl) { return Math.ceil(IN + chipTextW(fl.chip, LBL) + (fl.removed ? IN_XS + chipTextW(W("W-26", 0), CAP) : 0) + IN_XS + REMOVE_W); }
function filterChip(par, x, y, fl, D, k) {
  const w = filterChipW(fl), h = D.ctl, lw = chipTextW(fl.chip, LBL);
  box(par, x, y, w, h, { fill: "color.neutral.subtle", r: "radius.badge", name: "chip", k, role: "pill", sz: D.ctlTok });
  txt(par, fl.chip.replace(/ /g, NBSP), x + IN, cy0(y, h, LBL), lw, { type: LBL, col: SEC, k, name: "chip-label", deco: fl.removed ? "line-through" : undefined });
  let cx = x + IN + lw;
  if (fl.removed) { const rw = chipTextW(W("W-26", 0), CAP); txt(par, W("W-26", 0).replace(/ /g, NBSP), cx + IN_XS, cy0(y, h, CAP), rw, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k, name: "chip-removed-word" }); cx += IN_XS + rw; }
  const rx = x + w - REMOVE_W;
  box(par, rx, y, REMOVE_W, h, { r: "radius.badge", name: "chip-remove", k });
  gly(par, "✕", rx + (REMOVE_W - gw("✕")) / 2, y + (h - NV["size.icon.sm"] * 1.2) / 2, { col: SEC, k, name: "chip-remove-glyph" });
  return { x, y, w, h, fl };
}
// a toolbar control of the filter bar (CMP-001 secondary, D07's footer form); o.caret: a menu button's ▾
function barBtn(par, x, y, label, D, k, o) {
  o = o || {};
  const w = btnW({ t: label, caret: o.caret }, D);
  button(par, x, y, { t: label, caret: o.caret, name: o.name || "bar-button", kind: o.kind }, D, k);
  if (o.focus) ring(par, x - 3, y - 3, w + 6, D.ctl + 6, k);
  return { x, y, w, h: D.ctl };
}
// CMP-009 Rank by: its visible label "Rank by" and the select (the value and ▾), field-like (color.surface.app, the tertiary boundary)
function rankW(v, D) { return Math.ceil(chipTextW(W("W-9", 0), CAP) + IN_XS + IN + chipTextW(v, LBL) + IN_XS + gw("▾") + IN); }
function rankBy(par, x, y, v, D, k) {
  const lw = chipTextW(W("W-9", 0), CAP), sw = rankW(v, D) - lw - IN_XS;
  txt(par, W("W-9", 0).replace(/ /g, NBSP), x, cy0(y, D.ctl, CAP), lw, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k, name: "rank-label" });
  const sx = x + lw + IN_XS;
  box(par, sx, y, sw, D.ctl, { fill: "color.surface.app", stroke: TER, r: "radius.control", name: "rank-by", k, sz: D.ctlTok });
  drawParts(par, [{ t: v, type: LBL, col: PRI }, { g: "▾", col: SEC }], sx + IN, sx + sw - IN, y, D.ctl, D, k, "l");
  return { x, y, w: lw + IN_XS + sw, h: D.ctl, select: { x: sx, y, w: sw, h: D.ctl } };
}

// ---- the filter bar (§4.1): chips and Add filter flow left; the preset name (with Modified), Rank by and Presets ▾ right on the first row ----
function filterBar(par, x, y, w, D, o) {
  const k = K(), out = { chips: [], rows: 0 };
  const rGap = IN, rk = rankW(o.rank || W("W-9", 1), D), pw = btnW({ t: W("W-23", 0), caret: true }, D);
  const pn = o.preset ? chipTextW(o.preset.name, LBL) + (o.preset.modified ? IN_XS + chipTextW(W("W-25", 0), CAP) + 2 * IN : 0) + rGap : 0;
  const rightW = pn + rk + rGap + pw;
  let cx = x, cy = y, rowRight = x + w - rightW - rGap;
  const items = (o.filters || []).map((fl) => ({ fl, w: filterChipW(fl) })).concat([{ add: true, w: btnW({ t: W("W-6", 0) }, D) }]);
  for (const it of items) {
    if (cx > x && cx + it.w > rowRight) { cx = x; cy += D.ctl + IN_XS; rowRight = x + w; }
    if (it.add) out.add = barBtn(par, cx, cy, W("W-6", 0), D, K(), { name: "add-filter", focus: o.addFocus });
    else out.chips.push(filterChip(par, cx, cy, it.fl, D, K()));
    cx += it.w + IN_XS;
  }
  // the right group, row 1
  let rx = x + w - pw;
  out.presets = barBtn(par, rx, y, W("W-23", 0), D, K(), { caret: true, name: "presets", focus: o.presetsFocus });
  rx -= rGap + rk;
  out.rank = rankBy(par, rx, y, o.rank || W("W-9", 1), D, K());
  if (o.preset) { // the applied preset's name, with W-25's chip after any change (§10.2); its place is the board's (ambiguity 7)
    rx -= pn;
    const kp = K(), nw = chipTextW(o.preset.name, LBL);
    txt(par, o.preset.name.replace(/ /g, NBSP), rx, cy0(y, D.ctl, LBL), nw, { type: LBL, col: PRI, k: kp, name: "preset-name" });
    if (o.preset.modified) { const mw = chipTextW(W("W-25", 0), CAP) + 2 * IN, mx = rx + nw + IN_XS; box(par, mx, y + (D.ctl - D.pill) / 2, mw, D.pill, { fill: "color.surface.raised", stroke: "color.border.strong", r: "radius.badge", name: "modified-chip", k: kp, role: "pill" }); txt(par, W("W-25", 0), mx + IN, cy0(y, D.ctl, CAP), mw - 2 * IN, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: kp, name: "modified-label" }); out.modified = { x: mx, y, w: mw }; }
    out.presetName = { x: rx, y, w: nw };
  }
  out.bottom = cy + D.ctl;
  // §4.1 / §3.3: the derivation note under each estimated chip while it is a filter: W-3 or W-4's sentence then the link, on one line
  // where both fit inside the pane's content width; otherwise (r2, review r1 H2 / M3: W-3 plus the link is wider than the 784 px pane's
  // content box) the sentence on its own line(s), wrapped at the content width, and the link on the next line at the sentence's x
  let ny = out.bottom + IN_XS;
  out.notes = [];
  for (const ch of out.chips) {
    const cr = critOf(ch.fl.id);
    if (!cr || !cr.est || ch.fl.removed) continue;
    const s = cr.id === "regime" ? W("W-3", 0) : W("W-4", 0), link = W("W-3", 1), sW = chipTextW(s, CAP), lw = chipTextW(link, CAP), kn = K(), right = x + w;
    const nx1 = Math.max(x, Math.min(ch.x, right - sW - IN_XS - lw));
    if (nx1 + sW + IN_XS + lw <= right) { // one line, a follow row (the relayout's restack sets the link a space.1 after the sentence's MEASURED width)
      const fr = "dn" + (++DN_SEQ), lx = nx1 + sW + IN_XS;
      withF({ fr, fo: 0, fg: 0, flab: 1 }, () => txt(par, s, nx1, ny, sW, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: kn, name: "derivation-note", grow: "auto-width" }));
      withF({ fr, fo: 1, fg: IN_XS, flab: 1 }, () => txt(par, link.replace(/ /g, NBSP), lx, ny, lw, { type: CAP, col: LINK, k: kn, name: "derivation-link", deco: "underline", grow: "auto-width" }));
      out.notes.push({ x: nx1, y: ny, w: sW + IN_XS + lw, chip: ch, link: { x: lx, y: ny, w: lw, h: th1(CAP) } });
      ny += Math.ceil(th1(CAP)) + IN_XS;
    } else { // stacked: the sentence wraps inside the content box (its measured height), the link on its own line under it
      const nx = Math.max(x, Math.min(ch.x, right - sW)), bw = Math.min(sW, right - nx);
      const sh = Math.ceil(estLines(s, bw, TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP));
      txt(par, s, nx, ny, bw, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: kn, name: "derivation-note", ml: true });
      const ly = ny + sh;
      txt(par, link.replace(/ /g, NBSP), nx, ly, Math.min(lw, right - nx), { type: CAP, col: LINK, k: kn, name: "derivation-link", deco: "underline", grow: "auto-width" });
      out.notes.push({ x: nx, y: ny, w: bw, chip: ch, link: { x: nx, y: ly, w: lw, h: th1(CAP) } });
      ny = ly + Math.ceil(th1(CAP)) + IN_XS;
    }
  }
  if (out.notes.length) out.bottom = ny - IN_XS;
  return out;
}
// ---- CMP-028 callout above the grid (§2.2: not focusable, linked to the grid by aria-describedby); o.chip: CMP-239's chip at its head (§7.4) ----
function callout(par, x, y, w, tone, text, o) {
  o = o || {};
  const k = K(), pad = 6, gx = x + IN, chw = o.chip ? stalePillW(o.chip) + IN : 0, tx = gx + gw("i") + IN + chw, tw0 = x + w - IN - tx;
  const th = Math.ceil(estLines(text, tw0, TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP)), h = Math.max(D_NOTE, th + 2 * pad);
  const mark = tone === "warning" ? WARN_S : "color.status.info.default"; // §14: info.default (the badge pair) / warning.strong (its glyph and edge) on the subtle fill
  box(par, x, y, w, h, { fill: TONE[tone][0], r: "radius.control", name: "callout-" + tone, k });
  box(par, x, y, NV["border.thick"], h, { fill: mark, name: "callout-edge", k });
  if (tone === "warning") gly(par, "⚠", gx, y + pad + (th1(CAP) - NV["size.icon.sm"] * 1.2) / 2, { col: mark, k, name: "callout-glyph" });
  else { box(par, gx, y + pad + (th1(CAP) - 12) / 2, 12, 12, { stroke: mark, r: "radius.badge", name: "callout-glyph-ring", k }); txt(par, "i", gx, y + pad + (th1(CAP) - th1(CAP)) / 2, 12, { type: CAP, col: mark, align: "center", k: K(), name: "callout-glyph" }); }
  if (o.chip) stalePill(par, gx + gw("i") + IN, y + (h - PILL_H) / 2, o.chip, K(), "panel");
  txt(par, text, tx, y + pad, tw0, { type: CAP, col: PRI, k: K(), name: "callout-text", ml: true });
  return { x, y, w, h };
}
const D_NOTE = 24, PILL_H = 18;
// CMP-239 (D04): the dot and its words; on a stale row and at the head of the inputs-stale note (D08-C19: the panel-level chip)
function stalePillW(label) { return Math.ceil(IN_XS + 6 + IN_XS + chipTextW(label, CAP) + IN_XS + 2); }
function stalePill(par, x, y, label, k, where) {
  const w = stalePillW(label);
  box(par, x, y, w, PILL_H, { fill: STALE_SURF, stroke: STALE_IND, r: "radius.badge", name: "stale-chip" + (where ? "-" + where : ""), k, role: "pill" });
  box(par, x + IN_XS, y + (PILL_H - 6) / 2, 6, 6, { fill: STALE_IND, r: "radius.badge", name: "stale-dot", k });
  txt(par, label.replace(/ /g, NBSP), x + IN_XS + 6 + IN_XS, y + (PILL_H - th1(CAP)) / 2, chipTextW(label, CAP) + 2, { type: CAP, col: STALE_TXT, k, name: "stale-label" });
  return w;
}
// "Live only" (§6): D04-style text, a coverage label, not a state (R-4): a neutral CMP-011 tag, the words color.text.secondary
function liveTagW() { return Math.ceil(2 * IN_XS + chipTextW(W("W-12", 0), CAP) + 2); }
function liveTag(par, x, y, k) {
  const w = liveTagW();
  box(par, x, y, w, PILL_H, { fill: "color.neutral.subtle", r: "radius.badge", name: "live-only-chip", k, role: "pill" });
  txt(par, W("W-12", 0).replace(/ /g, NBSP), x + IN_XS, y + (PILL_H - th1(CAP)) / 2, chipTextW(W("W-12", 0), CAP), { type: CAP, col: SEC, k, name: "live-only-label" });
  return w;
}

// ---- the grid's columns (§8.1, in its order); the visible set: Symbol (locked, first), Last, every active criterion's column (forced, §8.1),
// the 24 h trend and Status, then the other columns in §8.1's order while the 784 px pane has room (CMP-056 shows or hides the rest: ambiguity 3) ----
const HEAD = { sym: "Symbol", last: "Last", chg: "24 h change %", turn: "Turnover 24 h", fund: "Funding %", oi: "OI Δ 24 h %", spread: "Spread bps", range: "24 h range %", z: "Tape speed z", regime: "Regime", trend: "24 h trend", status: "Status" };
const COL_ORDER = ["sym", "last", "chg", "turn", "fund", "oi", "spread", "range", "z", "regime", "trend", "status"];
const CELL_PAD = IN, SPARK_W = 56, SPARK_H = 14;
const dash = "—";
function cellText(r, id) { // the cell's value string (§8.2 formats as strings; "—" for a live-only row's skipped criterion, §6)
  if (id === "sym" || id === "last" || id === "turn" || id === "fund" || id === "spread" || id === "range") return r[id];
  if (id === "chg" || id === "oi" || id === "z") return r[id] == null ? dash : r[id];
  if (id === "regime") return r.regime == null ? dash : r.regime;
  return "";
}
const signGlyph = (v) => (/^\+/.test(v) ? "▲" : /^[−-]/.test(v) ? "▼" : null); // §8.2: zero takes no sign and no glyph
function colW(id, rows, o) {
  const hw = chipTextW(HEAD[id], CAP) + (id === "z" || id === "regime" ? IN_XS + badgeW() : 0) + (o.sort && o.sort.col === id ? IN_XS + gw("↓") : 0);
  let vw = 0;
  if (id === "trend") vw = SPARK_W;
  else if (id === "status") vw = Math.max(chipTextW(HEAD.status, CAP), ...rows.map((r) => (o.stale ? stalePillW(W("W-14", 0)) + (r.live ? IN_XS : 0) : 0) + (r.live ? liveTagW() : 0)));
  else for (const r of rows) { const t = cellText(r, id), mono = id !== "sym" && id !== "regime" && t !== dash; vw = Math.max(vw, (mono ? Math.ceil(Array.from(t).length * TYP(NUMS).size * EM_MONO) : chipTextW(t, id === "sym" ? LBL : CAP)) + ((id === "chg" || id === "oi") && signGlyph(t) ? gw("▲") + IN_XS : 0)); }
  return Math.ceil(Math.max(hw, vw) + 2 * CELL_PAD);
}
function columns(rows, o, avail) {
  const act = new Set((o.filters || []).filter((x) => !x.removed).map((x) => x.id));
  const must = new Set(["sym", "last", "trend", "status"].concat(Array.from(act)));
  const ws = {}; for (const id of COL_ORDER) ws[id] = colW(id, rows, o);
  let used = Array.from(must).reduce((a, id) => a + ws[id], 0);
  const show = new Set(must), hidden = [];
  for (const id of COL_ORDER) { if (show.has(id)) continue; if (used + ws[id] <= avail) { show.add(id); used += ws[id]; } else hidden.push(id); }
  const cols = COL_ORDER.filter((id) => show.has(id)).map((id) => ({ id, w: ws[id] }));
  cols[cols.length - 1].w += Math.max(0, avail - used); // the slack to the last column (Status, fixed last)
  if (used > avail + 0.5) warn("grid", CUR_F + ": the forced columns need " + Math.ceil(used) + " px in " + avail);
  return { cols, hidden };
}
// the sparkline (CMP-024, §8.3): 24 samples of a recorded window as display geometry only (D08-C8), static, colorBySign off, so the stroke
// is color.text.tertiary (15:278) at CMP-024's 1.5 px; drawn as horizontal and vertical runs (this script draws no path: pp/241's form)
function sparkline(par, x, y, w, h, sp, k) {
  const v = sp.v.map(Number), lo = Math.min(...v), hi = Math.max(...v), n = v.length, step = w / (n - 1), sw = 1.5;
  const py = (val) => y + (hi === lo ? h / 2 : h - ((val - lo) / (hi - lo)) * h) - sw / 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
    const x0 = x + i * step, y0 = py(v[i]), y1 = py(v[i + 1]);
    box(par, r2(x0), r2(y0), r2(step + sw), sw, { fill: TER, name: "sparkline-run", k });
    if (Math.abs(y1 - y0) >= 1) box(par, r2(x0 + step), r2(Math.min(y0, y1)), sw, r2(Math.abs(y1 - y0) + sw), { fill: TER, name: "sparkline-run", k });
  }
}
// the grid (CMP-049, role="grid"): the header row (color.surface.hover, color.text.primary), rows alternating color.surface.canvas (odd, the
// pane's own fill) and color.surface.raised (even); a stale scan shades every row color.data-confidence.stale.surface (D04:42) and its words
// take .stale.text; o.sort: { col, dir } marks one header (aria-sort, ↓ descending); o.focusRow: the row with the 2 px ring
function grid(par, x, y, w, h, rows, D, o) {
  const k = K(), { cols, hidden } = columns(rows, o, w), out = { cols, hidden, rows: [], head: {} };
  box(par, x, y, w, ROW_H, { fill: "color.surface.hover", name: "grid-head", k });
  let cx = x;
  for (const cl of cols) {
    const hk = K(), est = cl.id === "z" || cl.id === "regime", right = cl.id !== "sym" && cl.id !== "regime" && cl.id !== "trend" && cl.id !== "status" && !est;
    const parts = [{ t: HEAD[cl.id], type: CAP, col: PRI }];
    if (o.sort && o.sort.col === cl.id) parts.push({ g: "↓", col: PRI });
    box(par, cx, y, cl.w, ROW_H, { name: "grid-head-" + cl.id, k: hk });
    const tw0 = drawParts(par, parts, cx + CELL_PAD, cx + cl.w - CELL_PAD - (est ? IN_XS + badgeW() : 0), y, ROW_H, D, hk, right ? "r" : "l");
    if (est) { const bx = cx + CELL_PAD + chipTextW(HEAD[cl.id], CAP) + IN_XS; estBadge(par, bx, y + (ROW_H - PILL_H) / 2, PILL_H, hk); EST.push({ frame: CUR_F, what: HEAD[cl.id] + " header", host: { x: cx, y, w: cl.w, h: ROW_H }, kind: "header" }); }
    out.head[cl.id] = { x: cx, y, w: cl.w, h: ROW_H, tw: tw0 };
    cx += cl.w;
  }
  hair(par, x, y + ROW_H - 1, w, 1, "color.border.subtle", "grid-head-rule", k);
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor((h - ROW_H) / ROW_H));
  rows.slice(0, n).forEach((r, i) => {
    const ry = y + ROW_H + i * ROW_H, rk = K(), prev = STALE;
    if (o.stale) box(par, x, ry, w, ROW_H, { fill: STALE_SURF, name: "row-stale", k: rk });
    else if (i % 2 === 1) box(par, x, ry, w, ROW_H, { fill: "color.surface.raised", name: "row-even", k: rk });
    STALE = !!o.stale;
    try {
      let rx = x;
      for (const cl of cols) {
        const t = cellText(r, cl.id), right = cl.id !== "sym" && cl.id !== "regime" && cl.id !== "trend" && cl.id !== "status", x0 = rx + CELL_PAD, x1 = rx + cl.w - CELL_PAD;
        if (cl.id === "trend") sparkline(par, x0, ry + (ROW_H - SPARK_H) / 2, Math.min(SPARK_W, x1 - x0), SPARK_H, r.spark, rk);
        else if (cl.id === "status") {
          let sx = x0;
          if (o.stale) { sx += stalePill(par, sx, ry + (ROW_H - PILL_H) / 2, W("W-14", 0), rk) + IN_XS; } // §8.1: "Stale 15s", then "Live only"; both when both apply
          if (r.live) liveTag(par, sx, ry + (ROW_H - PILL_H) / 2, rk);
        } else if (t === dash) drawParts(par, [{ t: dash, type: CAP, col: SEC, name: "cell-not-evaluated" }], x0, x1, ry, ROW_H, D, rk, right ? "r" : "l");
        else if (cl.id === "sym") drawParts(par, [{ t, type: LBL, col: PRI, name: "cell-symbol" }], x0, x1, ry, ROW_H, D, rk, "l");
        else if (cl.id === "regime") drawParts(par, [{ t, type: CAP, col: PRI, name: "cell" }], x0, x1, ry, ROW_H, D, rk, "l");
        else {
          const g = (cl.id === "chg" || cl.id === "oi") ? signGlyph(t) : null; // ▲ / ▼ in color.buy / .sell.default, the number color.text.primary (D07:568–569)
          drawParts(par, (g ? [{ g, col: g === "▲" ? BUY : SELL }] : []).concat([{ t, type: NUMS, col: PRI, name: "cell" }]), x0, x1, ry, ROW_H, D, rk, "r");
        }
        rx += cl.w;
      }
    } finally { STALE = prev; }
    out.rows.push({ r, x, y: ry, w, h: ROW_H });
    ROWS8.push({ frame: CUR_F, filters: (o.filters || []).filter((fl) => !fl.removed), r });
    if (o.focusRow === i) ring(par, x + 1, ry + 1, w - 2, ROW_H - 2, rk);
  });
  out.shown = Math.min(n, rows.length);
  out.bottom = y + ROW_H + out.shown * ROW_H;
  return out;
}
// ---- CMP-026 inside the grid body (§9; SCR-151's anatomy, 14:2944: a heading, one sentence of cause, one action) ----
function gridState(par, x, y, w, h, D, kind) {
  const k = K(), s = kind === "failed" ? WD["W-18"] : WD["W-17"], gs = NV["size.icon.md"];
  const bw = btnW({ t: s[2] }, D), lh = th1(H_SM), dh = th1(BODY), total = gs * 1.2 + 8 + lh + 6 + dh + 12 + D.ctl, top = y + Math.max(24, (h - total) / 2);
  const cx = x + w / 2;
  gly(par, kind === "failed" ? "✕" : "▽", cx - gw(kind === "failed" ? "✕" : "▽", true) / 2, top, { big: true, col: kind === "failed" ? DANGER_S : TER, k, name: kind === "failed" ? "state-error-glyph" : "state-empty-glyph" }); // CMP-019: the scan-failed glyph color.status.danger.strong (§14), never the empty glyph (§9)
  const hw = twm(s[0], H_SM) + 4, dw = twm(s[1], BODY) + 4;
  txt(par, s[0], cx - hw / 2, top + gs * 1.2 + 8, hw, { type: H_SM, col: PRI, align: "center", k, name: "state-heading" });
  txt(par, s[1], cx - dw / 2, top + gs * 1.2 + 8 + lh + 6, dw, { type: BODY, col: SEC, align: "center", k, name: "state-sentence" });
  const by = top + gs * 1.2 + 8 + lh + 6 + dh + 12;
  button(par, cx - bw / 2, by, { t: s[2], name: kind === "failed" ? "state-retry" : "state-clear" }, D, k);
  return { bottom: by + D.ctl, action: { x: cx - bw / 2, y: by, w: bw, h: D.ctl } };
}
// ---- the footer (§2.2 item 4): the count, the age (W-13), Paused, the sort hold (W-15) with Re-sort, Pause updates (aria-pressed),
// Send matches to watchlist… (T2 for a Viewer, E09-D09:114); o.line: a footer line above the controls (W-8, W-19) ----
const FOOT_H = (o) => PD().ctl + 2 * IN_XS + (o && (o.line || o.sendReason || o.hold) ? Math.ceil(th1(CAP)) + IN_XS : 0); // a footer line, a T2 reason (2a) over its control, or (with the sort hold) the count and age
function footer(par, x, y, w, D, o) {
  const k = K(), h = FOOT_H(o), out = {};
  box(par, x, y, w, h, { fill: "color.surface.canvas", name: "pane-footer", k });
  hair(par, x, y, w, 1, "color.border.subtle", "pane-footer-rule", k);
  let cy = y + IN_XS;
  if (o.line) { txt(par, o.line, x + IN, cy, w - 2 * IN, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: K(), name: "footer-line" }); out.line = { x: x + IN, y: cy }; }
  if (o.sendReason) { const rw = chipTextW(o.sendReason, CAP); txt(par, o.sendReason, x + w - IN - rw, cy, rw, { type: CAP, col: SEC, align: "right", k: K(), name: "send-reason" }); } // E09-D09 2a: the reason beside its control (here over it, the footer row being full)
  const ly = cy;
  if (o.line || o.sendReason || o.hold) cy += Math.ceil(th1(CAP)) + IN_XS;
  if (o.noSend) out.noSend = true;
  // right group first (its width bounds the left words)
  let rx = x + w - IN;
  const send = W("W-28", 0), sendT2 = !!o.sendReason, sk = K();
  if (!o.noSend) {
    const sw = btnW({ t: send, kind: sendT2 ? "t2" : "secondary" }, D);
    rx -= sw;
    button(par, rx, cy, { t: send, kind: sendT2 ? "t2" : "secondary", name: sendT2 ? "write-send-matches-t2" : "write-send-matches" }, D, sk);
    if (sendT2) T2S.push({ frame: CUR_F, x: rx, y: cy, w: sw, h: D.ctl, labelX: rx + NV["space.button.paddingX"] + D.icon + D.gap - 0.5, reason: o.sendReason, what: send });
    out.send = { x: rx, y: cy, w: sw, h: D.ctl };
  } else rx += IN; // no Send: Pause updates ends at the inset
  const pl = W("W-13", 1), pwid = btnW({ t: pl }, D);
  rx -= IN + pwid;
  box(par, rx, cy, pwid, D.ctl, { fill: o.paused ? "color.action.selected" : "color.action.secondary.default", stroke: o.paused ? "color.border.selected" : null, r: "radius.control", name: o.paused ? "pause-toggle-pressed" : "pause-toggle", k: sk, sz: D.ctlTok });
  drawParts(par, [{ t: pl, type: LBL, col: PRI, al: "center", safe: true }], rx, rx + pwid, cy, D.ctl, D, sk, "c");
  out.pause = { x: rx, y: cy, w: pwid, h: D.ctl };
  if (o.hold) { // D07 D5: "Sort: {header} ▾ · paused" and Re-sort while the grid holds the order
    const rw = btnW({ t: W("W-15", 2), kind: "ghost" }, D);
    rx -= IN + rw;
    button(par, rx, cy, { t: W("W-15", 2), kind: "ghost", stroke: "color.border.default", name: "re-sort" }, D, sk);
    const ht = W("W-15", 0) + " · " + W("W-15", 1), hw = chipTextW(ht, CAP);
    rx -= IN_XS + hw;
    txt(par, ht, rx, cy0(cy, D.ctl, CAP), hw, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: K(), name: "sort-hold" });
  }
  // left: the count (color.text.primary), then the age and Paused (color.text.secondary)
  const kl = K(), left = [];
  if (o.count) left.push({ t: o.count, type: CAP, col: PRI, name: "count" });
  if (o.age) left.push({ t: (o.count ? "· " : "") + o.age + (o.paused ? " · " + W("W-13", 2) : ""), type: CAP, col: SEC, name: "age" });
  if (left.length && o.hold) drawParts(par, left, x + IN, x + w - IN, ly, Math.ceil(th1(CAP)), D, kl, "l"); // the row of controls is full: the count and age on the line above it
  else if (left.length) { const lw = partsW(left, D); if (x + IN + lw > rx - IN) warn("footer", CUR_F + ": the footer's words meet its controls"); drawParts(par, left, x + IN, rx - IN, cy, D.ctl, D, kl, "l"); }
  out.h = h;
  return out;
}

// ---- the pane: o = { focused, filters, rank, preset, notes: [{ tone, text, chip }], caption, rows, sort, stale, focusRow, state ("empty" |
// "failed"), foot: { count, age, paused, hold, line, sendReason }, addFocus, presetsFocus } ----
function scanPane(f, r, D, o) {
  const P = { kind: "Scanner", focused: o.focused !== false }; // the E15 chassis header; the kind word for the pane kind D08-G10 asks for (no `scanner` `PaneKind` at the base; ambiguity 1)
  const q = dockPanel(f, r, P, D), out = { q, r };
  box(f, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, { name: "scanner-pane", k: K() }); // no fill: the pane's extent, which no annotation may meet (d8Checks rule 1)
  const x = q.x + IN, w = q.w - 2 * IN;
  const fb = filterBar(f, x, q.y + IN_XS, w, D, o);
  out.fb = fb;
  let y = fb.bottom + IN;
  out.notes = [];
  for (const n of o.notes || []) { const c0 = callout(f, x, y, w, n.tone, n.text, { chip: n.chip }); out.notes.push(Object.assign({ n }, c0)); y += c0.h + IN_XS; }
  if (o.caption) { const ch = txt(f, o.caption, x, y, w, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: K(), name: "grid-caption", ml: true }); out.caption = { x, y, w, h: ch.h }; y += ch.h + IN_XS; }
  const fh = FOOT_H(o.foot);
  const gy = y, gh = q.y + q.h - fh - gy;
  out.grid = { x: q.x, y: gy, w: q.w, h: gh };
  if (o.state) { // the header kept, the state in the body (CMP-049's empty / error forms)
    const g = grid(f, q.x, gy, q.w, ROW_H, o.rows || RES, D, o);
    out.cols = g.cols; out.state = gridState(f, q.x, gy + ROW_H, q.w, gh - ROW_H, D, o.state);
  } else {
    const g = grid(f, q.x, gy, q.w, gh, o.rows || RES, D, o);
    out.g = g; out.cols = g.cols;
  }
  out.foot = footer(f, q.x, q.y + q.h - fh, q.w, D, o.foot || {});
  out.footY = q.y + q.h - fh;
  return out;
}

// ---- overlays ----
// CMP-041 menu: color.surface.overlay, color.border.default, radius.card; items { t, t2 (the reason, E09-D09 2a: lock + full label
// color.text.tertiary, the reason a second line), sep, check, est (CMP-227's look embedded after the label), focus, name }
function menu8(par, x, y, w, items, o) {
  o = o || {};
  const D = PD(), k = K(), pad = IN, itemH = D.ctl + IN_XS, inner = w - 2 * pad - D.icon - D.gap;
  const hs = items.map((it) => (it.sep ? 2 * IN_XS + 1 : it.t2 ? Math.ceil(th1(BODY) + estLines(it.t2, inner, TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP) + 3 * IN_XS) : itemH));
  const h = hs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + 2 * IN_XS, out = { x, y, w, h, items: [] };
  box(par, x, y, w, h, { fill: "color.surface.overlay", stroke: "color.border.default", r: "radius.card", name: o.name || "menu", k, bg: true });
  let cy = y + IN_XS;
  items.forEach((it, i) => {
    const ih = hs[i], ik = K();
    if (it.sep) { hair(par, x + pad, cy + IN_XS, w - 2 * pad, 1, "color.border.default", "menu-separator", ik); cy += ih; return; }
    const nm = it.name || (it.est ? "option-estimated" : "menu-item");
    box(par, x + 2, cy, w - 4, ih, { name: nm + (it.t2 ? "-t2" : ""), k: ik });
    const lx = x + pad + D.icon + D.gap;
    if (it.t2) {
      lockIcon(par, x + pad, cy + IN_XS + (th1(BODY) - D.icon) / 2, false, TER, ik);
      txt(par, it.t, lx, cy + IN_XS, inner, { type: BODY, col: TER, k: ik, name: "menu-item-t2-label" });
      txt(par, it.t2, lx, cy + IN_XS + th1(BODY), inner, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: ik, name: "menu-item-reason", ml: true });
      T2S.push({ frame: CUR_F, x: x + 2, y: cy, w: w - 4, h: ih, labelX: lx, reason: it.t2, what: it.t });
    } else {
      if (it.check) gly(par, "✓", x + pad, cy + (ih - NV["size.icon.sm"] * 1.2) / 2, { col: PRI, k: ik, name: "menu-check" });
      const lw = chipTextW(it.t, BODY);
      txt(par, it.t.replace(/ /g, NBSP), lx, cy0(cy, ih, BODY), lw, { type: BODY, col: it.col || PRI, k: ik, name: it.est != null ? "option-label" : "menu-item-label" });
      if (it.est) { estBadge(par, lx + lw + IN_XS, cy + (ih - PILL_H) / 2, PILL_H, ik); EST.push({ frame: CUR_F, what: it.t + " option", host: { x: x + 2, y: cy, w: w - 4, h: ih }, kind: "option" }); }
    }
    if (it.focus) ring(par, x + 2, cy, w - 4, ih, ik); // the 2 px inset ring (D07: menus never take color.surface.selected)
    out.items.push({ t: it.t, x: x + 2, y: cy, w: w - 4, h: ih });
    cy += ih;
  });
  return out;
}
const menuW = (items, extra) => Math.ceil(2 * IN + PD().icon + PD().gap + Math.max(...items.filter((it) => !it.sep).map((it) => (it.t2 ? 0 : chipTextW(it.t, BODY)) + (it.est ? IN_XS + badgeW() : 0))) + (extra || 0));
// CMP-017 popover, the range editor (§4.1): a dialog named "{criterion} range" (W-6), Min and Max CMP-008 fields with the unit suffix
// (color.surface.app, the boundary color.text.tertiary), Apply (CMP-001 primary); o.err: W-7 inline (the ✕ and the invalid boundary
// color.status.danger.strong, the words color.text.primary, §4.2); o.focus: "min" | "max"
const POP_W = 264;
function rangeEditor(par, x, y, o) {
  const D = PD(), pad = NV["space.panel.padding"], k = K(), out = { x, y, w: POP_W };
  const card = box(par, x, y, POP_W, 40, { fill: "color.surface.overlay", stroke: "color.border.default", r: "radius.card", name: "popover", k, bg: true });
  let cy = y + pad;
  const tk = K();
  txt(par, o.crit, x + pad, cy, POP_W - 2 * pad, { type: H_SM, col: PRI, k: tk, name: "popover-title" });
  cy += Math.ceil(th1(H_SM)) + IN;
  const lw = Math.max(chipTextW(W("W-6", 1), CAP), chipTextW(W("W-6", 2), CAP)) + IN, fw = POP_W - 2 * pad - lw;
  for (const [lab, val, key] of [[W("W-6", 1), o.min, "min"], [W("W-6", 2), o.max, "max"]]) {
    const fk = K(), bad = o.err && o.err === key, fx = x + pad + lw;
    txt(par, lab, x + pad, cy0(cy, D.ctl, CAP), lw - IN, { type: CAP, col: SEC, k: fk, name: "field-label" });
    box(par, fx, cy, fw, D.ctl, { fill: "color.surface.app", stroke: bad ? DANGER_S : TER, bw: bad ? "border.thick" : "border.hairline", r: "radius.control", name: "field-" + key, k: fk, sz: D.ctlTok });
    const uw = chipTextW(o.unit, CAP);
    if (val) txt(par, val, fx + IN, cy0(cy, D.ctl, NUMS), fw - 3 * IN - uw, { type: NUMS, col: PRI, k: fk, name: "field-value" });
    txt(par, o.unit, fx + fw - IN - uw, cy0(cy, D.ctl, CAP), uw, { type: CAP, col: TER, k: fk, name: "field-unit" });
    if (o.focus === key) ring(par, fx - 3, cy - 3, fw + 6, D.ctl + 6, fk);
    out[key] = { x: fx, y: cy, w: fw, h: D.ctl };
    cy += D.ctl + IN;
  }
  if (o.err) { // §4.2: inline, naming the filter; aria-invalid; announced once
    const ek = K(), s = W7(o.crit), ew = POP_W - 2 * pad - gw("✕") - IN_XS, eh = Math.ceil(estLines(s, ew, TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP));
    gly(par, "✕", x + pad, cy + (th1(CAP) - NV["size.icon.sm"] * 1.2) / 2, { col: DANGER_S, k: ek, name: "error-glyph" });
    txt(par, s, x + pad + gw("✕") + IN_XS, cy, ew, { type: CAP, col: PRI, k: ek, name: "error-text", ml: true });
    out.error = { x: x + pad, y: cy, h: eh };
    cy += eh + IN;
  }
  const aw = btnW({ t: W("W-6", 3) }, D);
  button(par, x + POP_W - pad - aw, cy, { t: W("W-6", 3), kind: "primary", name: "apply" }, D, K());
  out.apply = { x: x + POP_W - pad - aw, y: cy, w: aw, h: D.ctl };
  cy += D.ctl + pad;
  out.h = Math.ceil(cy - y); card.resize(POP_W, out.h);
  out.bottom = y + out.h; out.right = x + POP_W;
  return out;
}
// the modal scrim under a dialog (pp/361 r3's extent: below the TopBar to above the StatusBar; the banner band and the bars never scrimmed)
function modalScrim(f, wx, wy) { scrim(f, wx, wy + TOPBAR_H, VW, VH - TOPBAR_H - STATUSBAR_H); }
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the frames: windows (the live workspace with the SCR-104 pane docked) ----
// A window is the 1280 × 800 frame (§0, CITE:viewport) holding E15-D03's merged chrome (b5Shell: TopBar, NavRail, RightRail strip, tab
// strip, StatusBar; the role shells of 42-d03) and the dock: the scanner pane at D07's 784 px reference width (CITE:ref-width, D07:164),
// full dock height, then CMP-081's gutter and a chart panel of the pane's sync group (Open chart sets its symbol, §11; 42-d03's b5Panel at
// its §3.6 header step). The chart panel's plot is the frame's annotation column: the PROPOSED stamps, the names and the W-16 footer.
// o = { role ("owner" | "viewer"), pane (scanPane's o), over (fn(f, pane, win) → overlay info: menus, the popover, dialogs), stamps: [[y | fn(pane, ov), text | fn(pane, ov), { plain, owner }]], illustrative (false: no market value drawn, no W-16 note) }
let LAST_WIN = null;
function winFrame(B, row, x, y, o) {
  o = o || {};
  const f = frame(B, row.frame, x, y, VW, VH);
  CUR_F = row.frame;
  const role = o.role || "owner";
  const sh = role === "viewer" ? b5Shell(f, x, y, VW, VH, { user: c("b5UserViewer"), t2Cluster: true, killT2: true, health: "states", admin: false, save: "perm", reason: c("b5PermSave") })
    : b5Shell(f, x, y, VW, VH, {});
  const D = sh.D, dk = sh.dock, out = { f, D, dock: dk, x, y };
  const pr = { x: dk.x, y: dk.y, w: PANE_W, h: dk.h }, cr = { x: dk.x + PANE_W + GUTTER, y: dk.y, w: dk.w - PANE_W - GUTTER, h: dk.h };
  splitterV(f, dk.x + PANE_W, dk.y, dk.h, "rest", K());
  const ch = b5Panel(f, cr, "chart", D, false, "LIVE", { arm: null }); // unarmed: the chart is the sync group's target only
  out.chart = ch;
  out.pane = scanPane(f, pr, D, o.pane || {});
  out.ov = o.over ? o.over(f, out.pane, out) || {} : {};
  const q = ch.q, sx = q.x + 12, sw = (q.plotW || q.w) - 24;
  const cols = [{ x: sx, w: sw }];
  let maxY = q.y + q.h - 8;
  if (o.illustrative !== false) { // D07:626's footer at the foot of the annotation column, reserved first (W-16)
    const t = W("W-16", 0) + " (D07:626): every symbol and value here; each sparkline a recorded fixture window (packages/fixtures, 2026-10-05), not the symbol's 24 h path";
    const h = Math.ceil(estLines(t, Math.floor(sw * 0.85), TYP(CAP).size, false, 400) * th1(CAP)), ty = maxY - h - 2, kk = K();
    box(f, sx - 4, ty - 2, sw + 8, h + 4, { fill: "color.surface.raised", r: "radius.xs", name: "stamp-chip", k: kk });
    txt(f, t, sx, ty, sw, { type: CAP, col: PRI, k: kk, name: "annotation", ml: true });
    maxY = ty - 2 - NV["space.2"];
  }
  const stamps = o.stamps || [];
  withStamps(f, cols, q.y + 40, maxY, () => stamps.forEach((s) => stamp(typeof s[0] === "function" ? s[0](out.pane, out.ov) : s[0], typeof s[1] === "function" ? s[1](out.pane, out.ov) : s[1], s[2])));
  LAST_WIN = out;
  return out;
}
const DEN = (B) => B.bd.density;
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the board's own checks (d8Checks; each hit is a warn(), so the mock fails) ----
// pp/240's d4Checks (itself pp/241's d5Checks), renamed and re-keyed to this board's shapes, plus the advice and estimated rules:
// 1. annot-clear: no annotation text (frame captions, notes, stamps, the footer and the a11y strip) meets a drawn dialog or control rect (the
//    scanner pane's whole extent, its chips and their remove buttons, Add filter, Rank by, Presets, the derivation link, a menu or its items, the popover and its
//    fields, Apply, a dialog and its buttons, the footer's controls, a state's action, the chrome's controls);
// 2. view-as: in a frame named view-as no write control is drawn enabled: every write-* rect ends -t2, the TopBar's FLATTEN ALL… /
//    CANCEL ALL… / FREEZE… are their -t2 forms, and SCR-124's banner line is drawn (this board set has no view-as frame: the rule stands
//    for a frame drawn on request);
// 3. no-path: no footer (and no other text) holds a path ("work/", or "/" + a name with a file extension);
// 4. t2: every T2 control (a row-menu item, Send matches to watchlist…) has its lock leading (a lock-body rect inside it, left of its label)
//    and its reason drawn in its frame;
// 5. advice: no text on the board holds an advice word, best or top (§13, R-3, E25-D01:95), copy, preset names and notes alike;
// 6. estimated: every option and grid header of an estimated criterion (Tape speed z-score, Regime) holds CMP-227's look (an
//    estimated-badge rect) inside its own rect (§3.3, AC1; the look embedded, never a nested button), and every option-label naming an
//    estimated criterion sits in an option-estimated row;
// 7. words: every frame draws its §16.1 Copy cell: each id's strings (§13's W-rows, filled; wordsFor) appear on the frame.
// 1b. (r2) no text that starts inside a scanner pane runs past its right edge (reported under annot-clear);
// 8. filters (r2): every grid row drawn on a frame with active filters passes each of them (live-only "—" cells not evaluated, §6).
const D8_ANNOT = /\/ (annotation|frame-caption|proposed-stamp|crop-label|footer|a11y-strip|board-title|eyebrow)$/;
const D8_CTRL = /\/ (scanner-pane|chip|chip-remove|add-filter|rank-by|presets|bar-button|menu|criteria-menu|row-menu|presets-menu|menu-item|menu-item-t2|option-estimated|write-[a-z0-9-]+|popover|field-min|field-max|apply|dialog|dialog-field|button|button-t2|pause-toggle|pause-toggle-pressed|re-sort|state-retry|state-clear|modified-chip|topbar-control|toolbar-button|search-trigger|user-menu|control-t2|flatten-all(-t2)?|cancel-all(-t2)?|freeze(-t2)?|kill-switch(-t2)?|tab-new|toolbar-more|toolbar-save-[a-z0-9-]+|viewas-exit|env-badge|icon-button)$/;
const D8_TB = /\/ (flatten-all|cancel-all|freeze)$/;
const D8_PATH = /work\/|\/[\w.-]*\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]{0,4}\b/;
const D8_STATS = [];
const nbsp = (s) => String(s || "").replace(/\u00A0/g, " ");
function d8Checks(B) {
  if (!REC || !REC.shapes) return;
  const rect = (s) => ({ x: s.v.x, y: s.v.y, w: s.v.width, h: s.v.height }), meets = (a, b) => Math.min(a.x + a.w, b.x + b.w) - Math.max(a.x, b.x) > 0.5 && Math.min(a.y + a.h, b.y + b.h) - Math.max(a.y, b.y) > 0.5;
  const inside = (a, b) => a.x >= b.x - 0.5 && a.y >= b.y - 0.5 && a.x + a.w <= b.x + b.w + 0.5 && a.y + a.h <= b.y + b.h + 0.5;
  const desc = (s, out) => { for (const ch of s.children) { out.push(ch); desc(ch, out); } return out; };
  const pre = TICKET.length + 3, nm = (s) => String(s.v.name || "").slice(pre);
  // 1. annot-clear
  const ctrls = REC.shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && D8_CTRL.test(s.v.name || "")).map((s) => ({ r: rect(s), n: nm(s) }));
  let hits = 0;
  for (const s of REC.shapes) {
    if (s.kind !== "text" || !D8_ANNOT.test(s.v.name || "")) continue;
    const r = rect(s), m = ctrls.find((c0) => meets(r, c0.r));
    if (m && hits++ < 8) warn("annot-clear", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the " + nm(s) + " \"" + nbsp(s.text).slice(0, 50) + "\" (" + [r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h].map(Math.round).join(", ") + ") meets the " + m.n + " at " + [m.r.x, m.r.y, m.r.w, m.r.h].map(Math.round).join(", "));
  }
  if (hits > 8) warn("annot-clear", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": " + (hits - 8) + " more annotation texts meet a dialog or control rect");
  // 1b (r2, review r1 H2): no text that starts inside a scanner pane runs past the pane's right edge (the derivation note and its link,
  // the chips, the cells, the footer); the frame captions and stamps sit outside every pane, so they are not read here
  const panes = REC.shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && /\/ scanner-pane$/.test(s.v.name || "")).map(rect);
  let edge = 0;
  for (const s of REC.shapes) {
    if (s.kind !== "text" || D8_ANNOT.test(s.v.name || "")) continue;
    const r = rect(s), pn = panes.find((p0) => r.x >= p0.x - 0.5 && r.x < p0.x + p0.w && r.y >= p0.y - 0.5 && r.y < p0.y + p0.h);
    if (pn && r.x + r.w > pn.x + pn.w + 0.5 && edge++ < 8) warn("annot-clear", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the " + nm(s) + " \"" + nbsp(s.text).slice(0, 50) + "\" ends at x " + Math.round(r.x + r.w) + ", past its scanner pane's right edge " + Math.round(pn.x + pn.w));
  }
  if (edge > 8) warn("annot-clear", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": " + (edge - 8) + " more texts run past a scanner pane's right edge");
  const frames = new Map();
  for (const fr of REC.shapes) if (fr.kind === "board" && fr.parent && fr.parent.seq === 0) frames.set(String(fr.v.name), fr);
  const textsOf = (names) => { const out = []; for (const n0 of names) { const fr = frames.get(n0); if (fr) for (const s of desc(fr, [])) if (s.kind === "text") out.push(nbsp(s.text)); } return out; };
  // 2. view-as
  let va = 0;
  for (const [n0, fr] of frames) {
    if (!/view-?as/i.test(n0)) continue;
    va++;
    const all = desc(fr, []), live = all.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && (/\/ write-[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(s.v.name || "") && !/-t2$/.test(s.v.name) || D8_TB.test(s.v.name || "")));
    if (live.length) warn("view-as", "board " + pad2(B.n) + " frame " + n0.slice(String(B.b.name).length + 3) + ": " + live.length + " write control(s) drawn enabled while viewing as (" + live.map(nm).join(", ") + ")");
    if (!all.some((s) => s.kind === "text" && nbsp(s.text) === c("b5ViewAs"))) warn("view-as", "board " + pad2(B.n) + " frame " + n0 + ": no SCR-124 banner line");
  }
  // 3. no-path
  for (const s of REC.shapes) if (s.kind === "text" && D8_PATH.test(s.text || "")) warn("no-path", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the " + nm(s) + " \"" + String(s.text).slice(Math.max(0, String(s.text).search(D8_PATH) - 30), String(s.text).search(D8_PATH) + 30) + "\" holds a path (a board names page, board, version and theme only)");
  // 4. t2: the lock leads and the reason is drawn
  const locks = REC.shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && /\/ lock-body$/.test(s.v.name || "")).map(rect);
  let t2n = 0;
  for (const t of T2S) {
    t2n++;
    const fn = B.b.name + " / " + norm(t.frame);
    const lk = locks.find((l) => l.x >= t.x - 0.5 && l.x + l.w <= t.labelX + 0.5 && l.y >= t.y - 0.5 && l.y + l.h <= t.y + t.h + 0.5);
    if (!lk) warn("t2", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the T2 \"" + t.what + "\" in " + t.frame + " has no lock leading its label");
    if (!t.reason) warn("t2", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the T2 \"" + t.what + "\" in " + t.frame + " has no reason");
    else if (!textsOf([fn]).some((x) => x.indexOf(t.reason) >= 0)) warn("t2", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the T2 \"" + t.what + "\"'s reason \"" + t.reason + "\" is not drawn in " + t.frame);
  }
  // 5. advice
  let adv = 0;
  for (const s of REC.shapes) if (s.kind === "text" && ADVICE.test(nbsp(s.text)) && adv++ < 4) warn("advice", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the " + nm(s) + " \"" + nbsp(s.text).slice(0, 60) + "\" holds an advice word (§13: \"Rank by\" and \"matches\", never an advice word)");
  // 6. estimated: CMP-227's look inside every estimated option and header
  const badges = REC.shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && /\/ estimated-badge$/.test(s.v.name || "")).map(rect);
  const ESTL = CRIT.filter((c0) => c0.est).map((c0) => c0.label);
  let en = 0;
  for (const e of EST) { en++; if (!badges.some((b) => inside(b, e.host))) warn("estimated", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the " + e.what + " in " + e.frame + " has no CMP-227 look inside it"); }
  const estRows = REC.shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === "rect" && /\/ option-estimated$/.test(s.v.name || "")).map(rect);
  for (const s of REC.shapes) {
    if (s.kind !== "text" || !/\/ option-label$/.test(s.v.name || "") || ESTL.indexOf(nbsp(s.text)) < 0) continue;
    const r = rect(s), host = estRows.find((o0) => inside(r, o0));
    if (!host) warn("estimated", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the option \"" + nbsp(s.text) + "\" is an estimated criterion drawn without its marker row");
    else if (!badges.some((b) => inside(b, host))) warn("estimated", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": the option \"" + nbsp(s.text) + "\" has no CMP-227 look inside it");
  }
  // 7. words: the row's Copy cell on its frame
  let wn = 0;
  for (const row of regRows(B.n)) {
    const names = [B.b.name + " / " + norm(row.frame)].concat(FRAME_EXTRA.filter((e) => e.board === B.n && e.row === row.frame).map((e) => e.name));
    if (!frames.has(names[0])) { warn("words", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": no frame " + row.frame); continue; }
    const all = textsOf(names).join("\n");
    for (const id of String(row.copy).split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => s && s !== "—")) {
      const ws = wordsFor(row, id);
      if (!ws) { warn("words", "board " + pad2(B.n) + " " + row.frame + ": no copy for " + id); continue; }
      for (const w of ws) { wn++; if (all.indexOf(w) < 0) warn("words", "board " + pad2(B.n) + " " + row.frame + ": " + id + "'s \"" + w.slice(0, 60) + "\" is not on the frame"); }
    }
  }
  // 8. filters (r2, review r1 H1): on a frame with active filters, every drawn grid row passes each of them (a live-only row's recorded-history
  // cell is "—", not evaluated, §6); the row's values are the ones its cells print
  let fn8 = 0, fbad = 0;
  for (const e of ROWS8) {
    for (const fl of e.filters) { fn8++; if (!rowPasses(e.r, fl) && fbad++ < 8) warn("filters", "board " + pad2(B.n) + " " + e.frame + ": the row " + e.r.sym + " (" + fl.id + " " + e.r[fl.id] + ") breaks the active filter \"" + fl.chip + "\""); }
  }
  if (fbad > 8) warn("filters", "board " + pad2(B.n) + ": " + (fbad - 8) + " more drawn rows break an active filter");
  D8_STATS.push({ board: B.n, controls: ctrls.length, viewAsFrames: va, t2: t2n, estimated: en, words: wn, panes: panes.length, rowFilterTests: fn8 });
}
const finish0 = finish;
finish = function (B, y, notes, a11y) { finish0(B, y, notes, a11y); d8Checks(B); T2S.length = 0; EST.length = 0; ROWS8.length = 0; };
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the boards (spec §0's table; §16.1's rows) ----------------------------------
// Every board lays its §16.1 rows out in one row, left to right (§0: walked left to right), 80 px apart; each frame's caption prints the
// row's cells (State, Components, Copy, Tokens, Status) and what the frame shows; the board's notes and its a11y strip follow. A builder
// maps each row's state (regState) to its drawing, so a twin (08, 09, 10) draws exactly its own rows in its own theme.
const A11Y = [
  ["Focus order", "filter bar (each chip, its remove button and its derivation note's link; Add filter, Rank by, Presets ▾) → a column's derivation note link → the grid (one tab stop, roving tabindex) → the footer (Pause updates, Re-sort, Send matches to watchlist…); the notes are not focusable, except a derivation note's link (§12.1)"],
  ["Names", "each chip W-5's spoken form; the remove button \"" + W("W-6", 8) + "\"; the popover \"" + W("W-6", 4) + "\", its fields \"" + W("W-6", 5) + "\" and \"" + W("W-6", 6) + "\"; an estimated option \"" + W("W-2", 0) + "\"; an estimated header its text, the word once, described by its derivation sentence; a live-only cell \"" + W("W-12", 1) + "\"; a sparkline role=\"img\" \"" + W("W-31", 0) + "\" (§3.3, §4.1, §6, §8.3)"],
  ["Keys", "arrows, Home / End, Ctrl+Home / Ctrl+End, PageUp / PageDown in the grid; Enter on a row opens its chart, on a header sorts; Space on a header sorts; Shift+F10 the row menu; Ctrl+C copies the symbol; Enter on a chip opens its popover, Esc closes it without applying; no single-letter key, Ctrl+F not taken (§12.1)"],
  ["Live regions", "none in the pane: the app's polite region speaks W-22 once after a user's change and once per state-class change, stale and inputs stale once more on exit; count drift, ages and re-ranks are silent, so polling speaks at most once per " + CADENCE + " s scan (§12.2)"],
];
const THEME_NOTE = (B) => "Theme: " + B.bd.theme + "; every ink is the theme's resolved token value (packages/ui/tokens at the spec head; §14), SCR-104 at the compact density (16:113; D07 D30), " + ROW_H + " px rows, docked at D07's " + PANE_W + " px reference width inside the " + VW + " × " + VH + " window, beside a chart panel of its sync group, in E15-D03's merged compact chrome.";
const COPY_NOTE = "§13's copy deck is PROPOSED unless a cite says verbatim (W-3, W-14's D04 label, W-16, W-30 verbatim); every value on the boards is illustrative (W-16).";
const NO_AUDIT = "Scans write no audit record, being reads (14:88); preset writes are audited server-side as scanner_preset.* (T09 Scope), PROPOSED in 24 (D08-G7); public market metadata only, no account-scoped column (R-5, §15.3).";
// the G-rows a frame's columns wait on (§0: "every board marks each column that waits on its field with the G-row it waits on"; RULED BQ-4)
const G2_COLS = { turn: "Turnover 24 h", fund: "Funding %", oi: "OI Δ 24 h %", spread: "Spread bps", range: "24 h range %", z: "Tape speed z", regime: "Regime", trend: "24 h trend" };
function gCols(p) { const ids = (p.cols || []).map((c0) => c0.id).filter((id) => G2_COLS[id]); return ids.map((id) => G2_COLS[id]).join(", ").replace(/, ([^,]*)$/, " and $1"); }
const gStamp = (extra) => [(p) => p.grid.y + 120, (p) => "Waits on D08-G2 (E08-T09 #4708): the " + gCols(p) + " columns have no ScannerRow field yet (22:21814–21832); drawn against runScanner as it stands plus that row (RULED BQ-4)" + (extra ? "; " + extra : ""), { plain: true }];
const STAMP_RANK = [(p) => p.fb.rank.y, "Rank by (§5.1): the server sort picks which " + CAPN + " rows come back, highest first, no direction control (D08-G3, #4708)", { plain: true }];
const STAMP_LIVE = [(p) => (p.notes[0] ? p.notes[0].y : p.grid.y), "§6 (RULED BQ-13): a symbol we do not record is included and flagged; the count splits while a recorded-history criterion is active", { owner: true }];

// ---- the panes the frames draw (§16.1's states; illustrative values, W-16) ----
const FS01 = [F.fund, F.chg, F.spread, F.regime]; // board 01: four filters, one recorded-history criterion (Regime), so W-12 shows and the count splits (§6)
const FS2 = [F.fund, F.chg]; // W-21's own example filters (§12.2)
const PANE01 = () => ({ filters: FS01, notes: [{ tone: "info", text: W("W-12", 2) }], caption: caption21(FS01), foot: { count: W("W-11", 3), age: W13(3) } });
const PANE2 = (o) => Object.assign({ filters: FS2, caption: caption21(FS2), foot: { count: W11(38), age: W13(3) } }, o || {});

// ---- Board 01 populated (and twin 08 light) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  "results/populated": {

    "W-13": [W13(3), W("W-13", 1)], // "Paused" is stale/paused's (board 06)
  },

const NAMES01 = [(p) => p.grid.y + 90, "Names: a live-only \"—\" cell \"" + W("W-12", 1) + "\"; a sparkline role=\"img\", e.g. \"" + W31(RES[0]) + "\" (W-31); each chip W-5's spoken form, the caption's clause", { plain: true }];

  } }),
  "results/row-menu-viewer": (B, row) => ({ row, draw: (x, y) => {

    const w = winFrame(B, row, x, y, { role: "viewer", pane: Object.assign(PANE01(), { focusRow: ROWI, foot: { count: W("W-11", 3), age: W13(3), sendReason: W("W-30", 0) } }), over: (f, p) => {
      const rr = p.g.rows[ROWI], items = [{ t: W("W-29", 0), t2: W("W-30", 0), name: "write-add-watchlist" }, { t: W("W-29", 1), t2: W("W-30", 1), name: "write-create-alert" }, { t: W("W-29", 2), name: "menu-item", focus: true }];

    return "the Viewer's shell (E15-D03: lee · Viewer, the TopBar's T2 cluster): Shift+F10 on " + RES[ROWI].sym + " opens the row menu (CMP-041): Add to watchlist… and Create alert… T2 (CMP-079: the lock leading, the full label color.text.tertiary, the reason color.text.secondary under it), Copy symbol enabled and focused; the footer's Send matches to watchlist… T2 with its reason; Open chart is row activation, never hidden" + ROWS_LINE(w);
  } }),
};

The pane is a workspace pane docked at D07's " + PANE_W + " px reference width (D07:164) beside a chart panel of its sync group: Enter on a row sets that group's symbol (§11, D07 D11).",
    "Board 01's ruled set is " + A("board01.rows") + " rows (RULED board01.rows); at " + ROW_H + " px under the filter bar, the note, the caption and the footer, the " + VW + " × " + VH + " window's dock shows the first rows of it, the grid virtualised above " + A("virtualise") + " rows (15:528), the count " + W("W-11", 3) + " (illustrative).     "Server scan (R-1): one GET /scanner every " + CADENCE + " s while visible, never overlapping; no WS subscription for its rows, so the " + A("conn-cap") + "-symbol connection cap is never approached (§15.2).",
    COPY_NOTE, NO_AUDIT, THEME_NOTE(B),
  ], A11Y);
};

// ---- Board 02 criteria-menu (and twin 10 high contrast) -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Object.assign(WSUB, {
  "criteria/menu": { "W-6": [W("W-6", 0), W("W-6", 8)] }, // Add filter and the chips' remove name; the range editor's names are board 03's
});
const critItems = (focusId) => CRIT.map((c0) => ({ t: c0.label, est: c0.est ? true : null, name: c0.est ? "option-estimated" : "option", focus: c0.id === focusId }));
const B2 = {
  "criteria/menu": (B, row) => ({ row, draw: (x, y) => {
    winFrame(B, row, x, y, { pane: PANE2({ addFocus: false }), over: (f, p) => ({ menu: menu8(f, p.fb.add.x, p.fb.add.y + p.fb.add.h + 4, menuW(critItems()), critItems("z"), { name: "criteria-menu" }) }),
      stamps: [[(p, ov) => ov.menu.y, "Names: the options \"" + W("W-2", 0) + "\" and \"" + W("W-2", 1) + "\" (W-2): the option's accessible name carries the word; it embeds the look, never the component: no role, no tabindex, the glyph aria-hidden, no nested button, no EstimatedBadgeHost (15:2675; A-12); the chips' remove buttons \"" + W("W-6", 8) + "\"", { plain: true }],
        [(p, ov) => ov.menu.y + 100, "§3.3 (RULED BQ-8): the badge follows the criteria descriptor's estimated flag (D08-G9, #4708), never a catalogue adjective; Funding is not badged until E24-D01's C7 is decided (D08-C5); CMP-227's tooltip cannot open in an option, so the derivation note stands in for it", {}],
        [(p, ov) => ov.menu.y + 190, "The focused option takes the 2 px color.focus.ring inset (the ring on a focused estimated option, focus.ring on status.info.subtle measured in every theme, §14); the menu keeps 15:450's keys", { plain: true }], gStamp()] });
    return "Add filter open: CMP-041 lists §3.1's " + CRIT.length + " criteria in order (W-1), Tape speed z-score and Regime with CMP-227's look embedded after the label (W-2, AC1); focus on Tape speed z-score; the pane behind on W-21's two filters";
  } }),
  "criteria/derivation-note": (B, row) => ({ row, draw: (x, y) => {
    const fs = [F.fund, F.regime, F.z];
    winFrame(B, row, x, y, { pane: { filters: fs, notes: [{ tone: "info", text: W("W-12", 2) }], caption: caption21(fs), foot: { count: W11(143, 38), age: W13(3) } },
